# Contraction and three chronological placements of an exact objective module

Project gd-exact-profile-20260926; worker max3; one analytical invocation67
after ended baseline66, ended ceiling67, attempted ceiling68. Steering:
TASK.md and master_guidance90afbd2617b3aa0a. Consult OFF.

Status: COMPLETE JOINT ANALYTICAL DRAFT, UNVERIFIED. Every new formula,
channel bound and counterexample below belongs to this single joint draft.
No formal submission is allocated or made; no new fact is asserted. The
original unrestricted exact-profile target remains OPEN.

Exactly THREE changed physical families are screened. All three pass the
global Huber, EVERY original single-cut asymmetric-Huber witness, and ALL
convex smooth quadratic tests at a level which would give a fixed original
relative improvement. No full-function-class upper bound is established.
The distinction is substantive: actual functions refute two proposed lift
shortcuts, and an explicit original formal vector refutes an incomplete
pulled-back array. These failures do not physically reject the three words.

## 1. Definitions, physical families and candidate level

All spaces are finite-dimensional real Euclidean spaces. For a nonnegative
physical word h=(h_0,...,h_(N-1)), W_N(h) denotes the supremum of

  2(f(x_N)-f(x_star))/(L||x_0-x_star||^2)

over every convex differentiable f with L-Lipschitz gradient and a minimizer
x_star, and every x_0!=x_star, under x_(t+1)=x_t-h_t grad f(x_t)/L.
Here L>0 is fixed and arbitrary. This is the original W=2R normalization.
Every construction below scales by multiplying its normalized function by
L, so the physical points and normalized payoff do not change.

Put lambda=1+sqrt(2), rho=log_2(lambda), A=A_m=lambda^m. Write

  q_0=(), q_(j+1)=(q_j,1+(lambda-2)lambda^j,q_j),
  zeta=zeta_m=(1+sqrt(1+8A))/2,
  e=e_m=A/zeta=(zeta-1)/2,
  p_m=(q_m,1+e).

The word q_m is the unchanged exact basic PRIMITIVE module, of length
2^m-1 and sum A-1. The word p_m is the exact basic OBJECTIVE module, of
length 2^m and sum A+e. Its objective mass is D_p=2A+zeta=zeta^2.
The identities zeta^2-zeta=2A and 2e^2+e=A will be used explicitly.

Set a_c=7/10, c_c=6/5, tau_c=3/5, b_1=1+a_c A, b_2=1+c_c A.
These coefficients, the module placement and every physical step are
fixed before f, x_0, any oracle value or any trajectory is observed.

  H_F=(p_m,b_1,q_m,tau_c,b_2,q_m,1),
  H_M=(q_m,b_1,p_m,tau_c,b_2,q_m,1),
  H_E=(q_m,b_1,q_m,tau_c,b_2,p_m).

The subscripts F,M,E mean that the one objective module occurs at the
front, middle or end. The actual lengths and actual Huber denominators are

  N_F=N_M=3*2^m+2,
  N_E=3*2^m+1,
  D_F=D_M=1+2sum H_F=49A/5+2e+21/5,
  D_E=1+2sum H_E=49A/5+2e+11/5.                         (1)

For F/M the module sums are 3A+e-2 and the four external steps have sum
19A/10+18/5. For E the module sums are the same and the three external
steps have sum19A/10+13/5. This proves (1) without suppressing a cap,
contraction or terminal unit step. Counting the cap inside p_m as another
bridge gives FIVE positions for F/M and FOUR for E, each below seven.

The candidate upper level is

  q_goal=10/(97A).                                      (2)

The uniformly quantified statement W_(N_X)(H_X)<=q_goal for one fixed
X in {F,M,E} and all sufficiently large m is UNPROVED. If it were proved,
Section4's upper benchmark comparison would imply

  B_(N_X)W_(N_X)(H_X)<96/97,

on an unbounded sequence of ACTUAL horizons. No such full-class conclusion
is inferred from the restricted screens proved in this draft.

These words differ from the excluded two-module unequal families and from
(q_m,1+4A/5,p_m,1+A,q_m,b_m). They change the two large coefficients,
insert a strictly subunit physical step, and actually change chronological
module placement. No p_m is put into a primitive slot by declaration.

## 2. Accepted inputs, recall, retrieval and status discipline

The only accepted mathematical inputs are

  04219554e41d9750: full original-space primitive/dominant composition
  identities and the UNCONDITIONAL original benchmark ray, doubling,
  adjacency and fixed certified table scope;
  1252b8823bb5a290: smooth-convex interpolation necessity and the full
  original-coordinate objective certificate/terminal-square reduction;
  b98bb807d514e80d: the exact global Huber and every original single-cut
  asymmetric-Huber lower witness.

No conditional improving-seed hypothesis is assumed. No finite seed,
arbitrary-template inflation theorem or new verified fact is supplied.

The FULL shared report488a49d87beacf62 was recovered and read. Its physical
counterexample findingd31cdad2ae00d2f6 has IDENTICAL complete evidence;
this equality was checked as a text comparison. Its typed-interface
findingdf674e154ade2cd0 and scoped dead_endae22a122420dec6a were recalled.
Earlier equal-block closure67a2cb0744398ddb was also recalled. These are
awareness, not theorem premises. In particular their new inequalities are
re-derived here when needed rather than imported as facts.

Plans F,M,E are respectively4ee333441ca00353,bf0e2b0afbdb126c,
b3c8059b18a036e4. They have no separate correctness status.

The retrieval stage is active_program; program: contraction with reordered
typed exact modules; missing mechanism: a full original-coordinate coupled
objective lift beyond binary joins; search mode: mutation. Two Matlas
searches returned the all-short-step self-contracted-sequence theorem and
unrelated results. The all-short-step hypothesis excludes our long physical
steps, so that result is not used. Web fallback identified the primary
Zhang--Jiang source https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.12395. The exact existing text
downloads/zhang-jiang-2410.12395.txt was read at Definitions2.4--2.7,
Theorems3.1--3.2 and both proofs. Its primitive and dominant definitions
were expanded and matched to the accepted042 identities. Its ConPP
hypothesis needs TWO primitive children; ConPD needs a primitive LEFT
child and dominant RIGHT child and its prescribed bridge. The present
module order/coefficients do not meet those composition hypotheses merely
because each individual module has a certificate. No external theorem is
imported as a proof brick and no external_refs entry is asserted.

Skills applied: query-memory, search-math-results,
obtain-immediate-conclusions, propose-subgoal-decomposition-plans,
direct-proving, construct-counterexamples and identify-key-failures.
Each new calculation is part of this jointly UNVERIFIED draft, including
the intermediate calculations used inside its arguments. Nothing is
promoted or adopted downstream as an accepted result.

## 3. Complete typed module resources and actual pullbacks

Normalize L=1 and x_star=g_star=f_star=0. For a finite word w of length r
keep the free starting vector z_0, EVERY original g_0,...,g_r and every
f_0,...,f_r, and substitute

  x_i=z_0-sum_(t<i)w_t g_t,
  Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2,
  V_w=2sum_(t<r)w_t(f_t-f_r)+sum_(t<r)w_t(1-w_t)||g_t||^2
                                      -s(s+1)||g_r||^2,
  s=sum_(t<r)w_t.

Accepted1252 gives Q_ij<=0 on every ordered pair of actual samples,
including both orientations, optimum samples and the unqueried terminal.

For a zero-diagonal finite array Lambda set L_flow=diag(Lambda*1)-Lambda,
where 1 is the all-ones column, T_ii=1, T_it=w_t for t<i and zero for t>i.
Set omega=(-2w_0,...,-2w_(r-1),2s)^T and

  M_prim=diag(w_t(2-w_t) for t<r,-s(s+2))+sym(L_flow*T).

Here sym(K)=(K+K^T)/2, and the pairing with a Gram matrix is the trace
pairing. Accepted042 proves the exact identity, under L_flow^T*1=omega,

  V_w+sum_(i!=j)Lambda_ij Q_ij
                     =<M_prim,Gram(g_0,...,g_r)>.       (3)

This cancels EVERY internal value and is on the entire original gradient
space. The primitive initial-vector row is zero. No actual equality
between gradients at coincident positions is imposed on formal vectors.

Here is the full q_j array from accepted042's primitive join, defined
symbolically and NOT executed. At a join from q_j to q_(j+1), put
A_j=lambda^j, d=(lambda-2)A_j, kappa=lambda^2. Embed the entire left child
array with factor1 and entire right child array with factorkappa. Let p be
the physical bridge query, z the parent terminal, and t range over every
right-child nonterminal query, whose actual step is w_t. Add

  Lambda_(t,p)+=(kappa-1)w_t,
  Lambda_(z,p)+=kappa-1,
  Lambda_(p,z)+=(kappa-1)d,
  Lambda_(t,z)+=(kappa-1)w_t.                             (4)

Contributions on the same original pair add. All other entries are zero.
Every coefficient is nonnegative. Since d(2A_j+d)=A_j^2 and
2A_j+d=lambda A_j, the entire parent remainder is the embedded child
remainders with weights1,kappa. The empty remainder is zero, so the full
original-space remainder is ZERO at every q_j. In particular V_(q_j)>=0
on actual trajectories. Length and sum follow by the same symbolic
induction: 2^j-1 and lambda^j-1.

For the p_m cap, let p be the original query after its q_m and z its
terminal. Take the nonnegative weights u=(q_m,A+1,zeta), sum u=D_p.
Write U_p=sum_i u_i g_i over EVERY query of this physical p_m. Then the
complete unscaled objective identity is

  ||z_0||^2-2D_p f_z
    +sum Lambda^q_ij Q_ij+4A Q_(z,p)+2sum_i u_i Q_(i,star)
                              =||z_0-U_p||^2.           (5)

It is the empty-right-child case of accepted042's dominant join. At a q_m
nonterminal node the finite flow -2(q_m)_i is cancelled by2u_i. At p the
flow is2(A-1)-4A+2(A+1)=0. At z it is4A+2zeta=2D_p. Thus EVERY local
value is cancelled except the required terminal coefficient. All finite
and optimum multipliers are nonnegative. After division by D_p, (5) is
a full PSD objective certificate with the retained rank-one square.

In a complete H_X, z_0=x_0-Delta, where Delta is the sum of ALL weighted
original gradients strictly before that p_m. Its square is therefore

  ||x_0-Delta-U_p||^2,                                  (6)

including every original prefix/internal gradient and the free x_0.
This is not a new independently normalized starting vector.

For clarity, p_m has no primitive resource. On the actual unit quadratic,
the endpoint of q_m is -1/A for m>=1. The base is q_1=(sqrt(2)); the
induction multiplies the two child endpoints by -(lambda-2)A_j. The cap
then gives endpoint1/zeta. On any actual unit-quadratic word,

  V_w=1-(1+s)^2 x_r^2,

by telescoping w_t(2-w_t)x_t^2=x_t^2-x_(t+1)^2. For p_m this yields

  V_(p_m)=-(zeta^2-1)^2/(4zeta^2)<0.                    (7)

Thus no primitive use of p_m is justified, even though (5) is exact.

## 4. Original benchmark comparison at each actual N

Use the unchanged ALL-split recurrences

  C_0=B_0=1,

Z_3, and

           <=lambda^(-j)(sqrt(B_(3*2^j))+3/100)^2,

Only the accepted fixed-table scope is used, not a recurrence run. Its

## 5. Every original Huber cut, not just the exterior positions

Acceptedb98 gives, for every original index j,

  K_j=(h_j-1)_+^2/[(1+P_j)^2(1+2T_j)],
  P_j=sum_(t<j)h_t, T_j=sum_(t>j)h_t,
  K_N(h)=max{1/(1+2sum h),max_j K_j}<=W_N(h).           (11)

The positive part is max(v,0). We now bound EVERY term in (11) for the

The global term satisfies

  1/D_X<5/(49A)<10/(97A),                              (12)

as485<490. The contraction and terminal unit step have zero cut term.

The exact first exterior-bridge cuts are

  F: (49/100)A^2/[(A+e+1)^2(32A/5+11/5)],

It remains to audit ALL q_m internal steps, including the q_m inside p_m.
Any such step is a level-r bridge1+(lambda-2)u with u=lambda^r,

its immediately following local child has sumu-1. Other previous or

For a cut inside the FIRST q_m, 1+P_j>=u, so its excess-to-prefix ratio

Each displayed interior bound is strictly below5/(49A). This covers each
original step without deleting indices or replacing a child by one jet.
Together with (12)--(13) and the exterior formulas it yields

  K_(N_X)(H_X)<=5/(49A)<q_goal, m>=16.                  (14)

For completeness every individual (11) witness is an ACTUAL whole-run
function. At a selected j with h_j>1 set c=1/(1+P_j),
d=c(h_j-1)/(1+2T_j), and define

  F_(c,d)(x)=-d*x-d^2/2, x<=-d;
             x^2/2,     -d<=x<=c;
             c*x-c^2/2, x>=c.

The derivative clip(x,[-d,c]) is continuous, nondecreasing and1-Lipschitz;
the function has unique minimizer0. Starting at1, all original prefix

x_i=-d(1+2T_j)+d sum_(j<t<i)h_t<=-d,
g_i=-d, f_i=-d*x_i-d^2/2. Every internal original sample and terminal

## 6. Whole-word quadratic screen for every curvature

For theta in[0,1] use F_theta(x)=theta*x^2/2, initial point1. Put
P_q(theta)=product_(w in q_m)(1-w theta), defined symbolically, never
executed. The EXACT complete quadratic endpoint products are

  H_E: P_q(theta)^3(1-b_1 theta)(1-tau_c theta)
                            *(1-b_2 theta)(1-(1+e)theta),
  H_F,H_M: the same product multiplied by(1-theta).     (15)

The physical ordering matters for the full nonlinear problem even though
scalar quadratic factors commute. No internal q step or objective cap is
removed from these products. The normalized quadratic payoff is theta
times their square. The final unit factor has magnitude at most1.

We derive a UNIFORM bound on (15) rather than test isolated curvatures.
For q_m, let X_t be its actual scalar unit-initial-point trajectory under
F_theta and s=A-1. Expanding the primitive energy and telescoping
h_t theta(2-h_t theta)X_t^2=X_t^2-X_(t+1)^2 gives exactly

  V_q=1-[1+s theta+s(s+1)theta^2]P_q(theta)^2
               -(1-theta)theta sum_(t<2^m-1)h_t X_t^2.

The full primitive certificate in Section3 gives V_q>=0. Thus

  P_q(theta)^2<=[1+(A-1)theta+A(A-1)theta^2]^-1.         (16)

This is an actual quadratic consequence of a complete primitive resource,
not a formal reduced-matrix positivity test.

Put delta_A=1-1/A, v=A theta, alpha_A=7/10+1/A,
beta_A=6/5+1/A, k_A=alpha_A+beta_A, r_A=alpha_A beta_A.
For A>=1000,

  19/10<=k_A<=191/100,
  21/25<=r_A<=17/20,
  delta_A^-3<101/100.                                  (17)

The last inequality follows from delta_A^3>=1-3/A>=997/1000 and
100000<100697. For the middle bounds use1/A<=1/1000, so
k_A<=1902/1000<191/100 and r_A<=701*1201/10^6<17/20.

The following polynomial identity is a symbolic positivity certificate:

 (1+v+v^2)^3-10v(1-k_A v+r_A v^2)^2
   =(1-7v+20v^2)
    +v^2[(20k_A-14)+(7-10(k_A^2+2r_A))v+20k_A r_A v^2]
    +v^4[6+(3-10r_A^2)v+v^2].                          (18)

For v>=0, the second bracket is at least24-47v+31v^2 using (17);
its discriminant is2209-2976<0. The third bracket is at least
6-(17/4)v+v^2, with discriminant289/16-24<0. The first bracket has
discriminant49-80<0. All three quadratics are strictly positive.
Therefore v(1-k_A v+r_A v^2)^2<(1+v+v^2)^3/10.

If0<=theta<=1/(1+e), the cap factor has magnitude at most1. The
contraction and possible final unit factors also have magnitude at most1.
Using (16), whose denominator is at leastdelta_A(1+v+v^2), and (18),
the complete quadratic payoff is at most

  1/(10A delta_A^3)<101/(1000A).                       (19)

If1/(1+e)<=theta<=1, then b_1,b_2>=1+e for A>=10000, since
e<=sqrt(A) and (7/10)A>=sqrt(A). Hence both exterior factors and the cap
are bounded in absolute value by b_1 theta,b_2 theta,(1+e)theta,
respectively. Now (16)'s denominator is at leastdelta_A A^2 theta^2.
The entire quadratic payoff is consequently at most

  [r_A^2*((1+e)^2/A)/(A delta_A^3)]
                                        *theta(1-3theta/5)^2.

The algebraic identity2e^2+e=A implies
(1+e)^2/A=1/2+3e/(2A)+1/A<13/25 for A>=10000.
Also r_A^2<3/4. Differentiating the cubic on[0,1] gives its maximum
20/81 at theta=5/9: its derivative is(1-3theta/5)(1-9theta/5), so the
function increases up to5/9 and decreases afterwards. It follows that
the last display is at most

  13/(135A delta_A^3)<101/(1000A),                      (20)

because130<135 and (17). The two intervals cover ALL curvatures, including
theta=0. Since m>=16 gives A>65536, all thresholds above hold.

The spectral decomposition of a real PSD quadratic with Hessian bounded
by the identity makes its normalized error a convex combination of these
scalar payoffs (zero-eigenvalue components have zero payoff). Thus the
ENTIRE convex L-smooth quadratic class satisfies, for every word,

  W_quad(H_X)<=101/(1000A)<10/(97A), m>=16.             (21)

Here W_quad denotes the supremum over that RESTRICTED class only. Its
comparison follows from9797<10000. No all-function W upper follows.

Combining (10),(14),(21), the Huber/single-cut channel ratio is at most
48/49 and the entire quadratic channel ratio is below606/625, both below
1, at the TRUE horizons. These explicit restricted inequalities show why
the earlier physical rejections cannot simply be recycled for these words.

## 7. Actual original unit-quadratic transcripts and intermediate costs

For one static function x^2/2 throughout, all original query jets are
x_t=product_(i<t)(1-h_i), g_t=x_t, f_t=x_t^2/2. This is a symbolic
full transcript; no product or trajectory was executed. The complete
module endpoint identities give the following consecutive endpoints.

  F: 1/zeta, -a_c A/zeta, a_c/zeta, (2/5)a_c/zeta,
                -(2/5)a_c c_c A/zeta, (2/5)a_c c_c/zeta, 0.
  M: -1/A, a_c, a_c/zeta, (2/5)a_c/zeta,
                -(2/5)a_c c_c A/zeta, (2/5)a_c c_c/zeta, 0.
  E: -1/A, a_c, -a_c/A, -(2/5)a_c/A,
                                      (2/5)a_c c_c, (2/5)a_c c_c/zeta.

Each endpoint is after the corresponding displayed module/step in Section1.
F/M have ZERO actual terminal loss on this quadratic, while E has
(4/25)a_c^2 c_c^2/zeta^2. The loss is bounded by (21).

F nevertheless has an intermediate point -a_c A/zeta directly after b_1;
M has an intermediate point -(2/5)a_c c_c A/zeta after b_2. Their squared
radii grow with A because zeta^2/A is bounded above. These are actual
whole-run instances with attained unique minimum0 and true initial radius1.
They refute any monotone transport of the earlier p_m objective value
through the later long steps, even though their complete terminal values
do not refute q_goal. A certificate cannot forget those gradients or assume
that a front/middle objective child becomes a primitive left child.

## 8. First direct lift attempt: fold the two-q prefix into a primitive

For E the apparent decomposition is a two-q prefix, the contraction,
the second bridge and the terminal p_m. Accepted ConPD would require that
prefix to be genuinely primitive. It is not. This is an ACTUAL function
counterexample, stronger than failure of a selected multiplier pattern.

Let w_pre=(q_m,b_1,q_m,tau_c), P=A-1, b=b_1,
T=A-1+3/5, U=P+b=17A/10. Take the SINGLE static asymmetric Huber
F_(c,d) from Section5 with c=1/A and d=1/(4A), initial point1.

On the first q_m every original prefix sample is x_i=1-cP_i>=c,
g_i=c, f_i=c*x_i-c^2/2. Its terminal query is x=c. The actual bridge b
sends it to-a_c=-7/10. At every original query in the next q_m and the
contraction step, write R_i for the exact sum of already taken steps in
that suffix. Then x_i=-7/10+dR_i and g_i=-d. At the prefix terminal,

  x_pre=-7/10+dT=-9/20-1/(10A)<=-d, A>=1.

Thus every displayed suffix query, including the contraction, lies on
the same negative affine piece. This explicitly preserves all original
queries and values; no source-free or endpoint reset occurs.

Here is a direct energy calculation independent of each child's internal
step distribution. For any constant-gradient left segment of sumP,

  2sum_left h_i(f_i-f_p)+sum_left h_i(1-h_i)c^2
                                                   =c^2 P(P+1).

This follows by writing f_i-f_p=c^2 sum_(left t>=i)h_t and using
2sum_i h_i sum_(t>=i)h_t=P^2+sum_i h_i^2. The constant-gradient right
segment of sumT contributes d^2 T(T+1) before the parent terminal term.
With f_p=c^2/2, f_pre=d a_c-d^2(T+1/2), and parent sumP+b+T, collect the
full V_w definition in Section3 to obtain

  V_(w_pre)=c^2[(P+1)^2-(b-1)^2]-2U d a_c-U^2 d^2
            =1-(a_c+U d)^2
            =1-(9/8)^2=-17/64.                        (22)

All f_i and gradients in this identity are the actual original jets of
the same function. Every ordered original interpolation inequality holds
by accepted1252, and the scalar Gram is PSD. A primitive certificate
with nonnegative original finite multipliers and full PSD would make
V_(w_pre)>=0, contradicting (22). Thus no primitive certificate for this
ACTUAL prefix exists, irrespective of its signs/support/rank choice.

The static function is well-defined through the rest of H_E. Equation(22)
only concerns the displayed prefix's primitive property. It does NOT give
a larger-than-q_goal complete terminal payoff for H_E, and it does NOT
reject H_E as an improving objective schedule. The joint draft does not
assign a closed-form later trajectory or terminal loss to that function.

## 9. What the contraction actually guarantees, and what it does not

Let x_+=x_- -tau_c g_-, g_+=grad F(x_+), and d_g=g_- -g_+.
The two ACTUAL original residuals obey

  Q_(-,+)+Q_(+,-)=||d_g||^2-tau_c<d_g,g_-><=0.

The complete, sharp local consequence is therefore the ball condition

  ||g_+-(1-tau_c/2)g_-||^2<=tau_c^2||g_-||^2/4.         (23)

For tau_c=3/5 its center is(7/10)g_- and radius(3/10)||g_-||.
The unchanged gradient g_+=g_- lies on its boundary. A unit quadratic
g_+=(2/5)g_- lies on the opposite boundary. In the actual affine suffix
of Section8, g_+=g_-=-d!=0. Thus replacing the contraction by an oracle
relation g_+=(2/5)g_- is false on this same initialized original prefix.

Equation(23) retains two distinct formal original gradients and both
ordered rows. It does not justify deletion, equality or fixed collinearity
in a uniform certificate. The quadratic screen used the exact scalar
quadratic factor; its successful damping does not transfer to the full
function class.

## 10. Second direct lift attempt: transport the terminal p_m by radius

For E, let Delta be the full prefix displacement before p_m and
y=x_0-Delta its ACTUAL starting point. Identity(5) gives

  D_p q_goal||x_0||^2-2D_p f_N+sum_module Lambda Q
      =D_p q_goal||x_0||^2-||y||^2+||y-U_p||^2
      =D_p q_goal||x_0||^2-2<x_0,U_p>
                                      +2<Delta,U_p>+||U_p||^2. (24)

The sum_module includes every q_m finite multiplier, the cap finite row
and every module optimum multiplier in (5). Its full finite value flow
is correct even in the parent:2D_p at N and0 at every other original
index. All signs are nonnegative. However its original-space quadratic
in (24) need not be PSD.

One attempted sufficient condition was the universal radius contraction
||y||^2<=D_p q_goal||x_0||^2. It fails on a complete ACTUAL H_E trajectory.
Take c=d=1/(10A) in the symmetric Huber F_(c,c), initial point1.
On EVERY original query of each of F,M,E, including cap, contraction,
last unit step and the terminal, the gradient is c: the smallest terminal
coordinate is above1/2 for m>=16, while c<1/2. Specifically,

  x_i=1-cP_i, g_i=c, f_i=c*x_i-c^2/2,
  x_N(E)=51/100-e/(10A)-3/(50A),
  x_N(F)=x_N(M)=51/100-e/(10A)-4/(25A).

These are full original-run formulas, with all internal step sums retained.
The unique minimizer is0 and the true initial squared radius is1. Before
the p_m of E, the actual prefix sum is39A/10+3/5, so

  y=61/100-3/(50A)>=11/20,
  ||y||^2>=121/400.

On the other hand zeta<=2sqrt(A) for A>=1, by evaluating
zeta^2-zeta-2A at2sqrt(A). For m>=16, zeta/A<1/100 and hence

  D_p q_goal=20/97+10zeta/(97A)<201/970<21/100<121/400. (25)

The claimed radius transport is false. Its actual FULL terminal normalized
payoffs, which are not improving-level counterexamples, are exactly

  E: 51/(500A)-e/(50A^2)-11/(500A^2),
  F/M:51/(500A)-e/(50A^2)-21/(500A^2),                 (26)

all belowq_goal because4947<5000. These formulas explicitly distinguish
failure of a proposed lift lemma from a physical rejection of H_X.

There is also a full formal-vector counterexample to the module-only array
in (24). Choose a nonzero vector v_0, x_0=0, one original prefix gradient
g_0=-v_0/h_0, all other prefix gradients zero, all internal p_m gradients
zero, and its ORIGINAL terminal gradient g_N=v_0/zeta. Here h_0=sqrt(2)>0
for m>=1. Then Delta=-v_0 and U_p=v_0, so the exact remainder (24) is

  -||v_0||^2<0.                                       (27)

No original coordinate is quotiented out. This is a FORMAL original-space
PSD test, not an actual interpolable transcript and not a terminal lower
bound. It refutes exactly the incomplete module-only lift with zero
prefix multipliers. The full array may add many nonnegative prefix and
cross-module rows, which were not present in (24). Such a repair has not
been constructed. The cross term2<Delta,U_p> and the original initial
mixed term-2<x_0,U_p> cannot be removed by pretending that y is a new origin.

## 11. Exact remaining full certificate obligation

The screened words require an ORIGINAL full objective certificate, not a
matrix involving only three block endpoints. For one chosen X and every
sufficiently large m, one must supply predetermined nonnegative original
ordered multipliers lambda_ij, indices{star,0,...,N_X}, with finite flow

  sum_j lambda_ij-sum_j lambda_ji=2*1_(i=N_X),

and prove PSD of the complete formal quadratic

  M_cert=q_goal||x_0||^2-2f_(N_X)+sum_(i!=j)lambda_ij Q_ij

on ALL independent original vectors(x_0,g_0,...,g_(N_X)), after the actual
GD position substitution. All internal values, caps and the contraction
are physical. There is no block-scaling assumption or imposed equality
between formal gradients. Every displacement and initial mixed term must
be paid. A uniform explicit rule for the growing arrays and its proof are
needed, not a separate finite-m matrix experiment.

Accepted1252 allows an equivalent exact route which keeps the ENTIRE
prefix. Choose alpha_j>=0 on J_pre={star,0,...,N_X-1}, sum_j alpha_j=1,
and complete nonnegative prefix multipliers with finite prefix flow
out_i-in_i=2alpha_i. Define

  V_alpha=x_(N_X)-sum_(j in J_pre)alpha_j(x_j-g_j),
  R_alpha=q_goal||x_0||^2
     -2sum_(j in J_pre)alpha_j(f_j-||g_j||^2/2)
     -||V_alpha||^2+sum_(i!=j in J_pre)lambda^pre_ij Q_ij.

Then the full identity is

  M_cert=||g_(N_X)-V_alpha||^2+R_alpha.                  (28)

Its full PSD is equivalent to PSD of R_alpha on ALL original prefix
vectors, since the free terminal gradient may be chosen V_alpha. Prefix
values cancel only after the exact displayed flow. The unused incoming
terminal multipliers may be zero, but every other original pair remains
available and no entire module is deleted. No explicit alpha/prefix array
with this PSD property is provided in this draft. Equations(24)--(27)
give a concrete unpaid direction any successful parent proof must handle.

For F and M the earlier p_m can supply its legitimate identity(5), but its
output value cannot be monotone transported through the later long bridge,
as Section7 demonstrates, and its primitive type fails by(7). Applying
(28) to their actual full words retains those internal gradients and all
remaining steps. It supplies a precise full-space problem rather than an
automatic objective-child migration. No valid full array was found for
F, M or E; none is claimed to exist from the successful channels.

## 12. Per-plan verdicts, scoped failures and handoff

F: exact length/mass/types, every original cut and all quadratics have
complete joint derivations. Its leading p_m is genuinely objective; a
later actual long-step quadratic query has growing objective value, so
forgetting its output and assuming subsequent monotone value flow fails.
The full original objective proof is PARTIAL/OPEN, not physically refuted.

M: the same complete physical channel audit passes at its TRUE horizon.
The actual middle objective module, contraction, second bridge and full
last q_m/unit suffix are retained. The intermediate quadratic cost after
b_2 and the nonprimitive p_m type block the corresponding two shortcuts.
The full original objective proof is PARTIAL/OPEN, not physically refuted.

E: the same complete channel audit passes at the different TRUE horizon.
The two-q-plus-contraction prefix is ACTUALLY nonprimitive, with exact
energy-17/64. The unconditional prefix-radius bound fails on a complete
weak-slope Huber trajectory. The exact module-only parent array has full
correct signs/flow but the negative original-space direction(27). The
full original objective proof is PARTIAL/OPEN, not physically refuted.

The scoped dead ends are precisely: replacing the3/5 contraction by the
unit-quadratic damping law on general functions; treating this E prefix as
primitive; discarding the original module starting position through a
uniform radius transport; using the p_m-only signs/flow array as if its
pullback were PSD; and transporting a front/middle p_m value monotonically
across the actual later long steps. These are failures of proof schemas,
not an impossibility theorem for a complete coupled original array.

The changed physical words therefore remain SCREEN SURVIVORS ONLY.
The highest-leverage remaining interface is a full original-space
nonnegative-row payment of Delta/U_p and initial-point mixed terms, with
exact value flow and uniform PSD atq_goal, or an actual complete-run
nonquadratic lower witness above the claimed level. A finite macro PSD,
virtual residual, total mass, scalar affine cut, or all-quadratic upper
does not answer that interface.

Resources used: ZERO mathematical programs, numerical/arithmetic replays,
executed trajectories/recurrences, solvers, optimizers/DP, installations,
consults, formal calls, new accepted facts or subagents. Filesystem reads,
text comparisons, JSON serialization and hashing are administrative only.
No program has evaluated any displayed mathematical formula.

This completes ONE authorized analytical invocation. A new planning round
requires a new dispatch; no budget/old grant is automatically reopened.
No original-Goal completion, finalization, revocation, publication,
shutdown, outward action or formal promotion occurs. The fact graph is
unchanged and the original unrestricted problem remains OPEN.

# C53: a formal template defeats the entire mixed-boundary repeated-copy join

Status: complete joint proof candidate, **not verifier-accepted**. No solver
or verifier call is allocated. The only mathematical input from the fact graph
is `1252b8823bb5a290`, for the interpolation convention and full formal-vector
certificate interpretation. The construction suggested by shared finding
`c7f10ed5dc99275b` is defined and expanded here; that finding is not a premise.

The conclusion concerns a specified recursively generated certificate family.
The formal vectors in this proof are NOT legal smooth-convex transcripts. In
particular, the conclusion below is neither a lower bound for unrestricted
W_n nor a decision of the original exact-profile problem.

## 1. The certificate family, including all its available parameters

Use smoothness one and optimum jet x_star=g_star=f_star=0. For every ordered
pair of distinct samples put

    Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2,
    C_ij=-2Q_ji
        =2(f_i-f_j)+2<g_j,x_j-x_i>-||g_i-g_j||^2.

The accepted fact implies C_ij>=0 on every legal trajectory. All identities
below, however, are identities in independent formal vectors after the GD
substitution; no interpolation constraints are imposed on test vectors.

Fix a>0,c>0 and define

    d_j=a c^j,
    H_0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+d_j,H_j),
    n_j=2^j-1, S_j=sum H_j, M_j=1+S_j.

The initial block in the certificate recursion is H_1=(1+a), not H_0.
For each block choose real u_j,v_j, with final v_m>0, and a multiplier array
lambda^(j) on its samples and star. Require the following star pattern:

    lambda_(star,i)=h_i             for every queried i<n_j,
    lambda_(star,n_j)=u_j,
    lambda_(i,star)=0               for i<n_j,
    lambda_(n_j,star)=s_j,
    s_j=S_j+u_j-v_j.                                      (1)

Here s_j is only an incoming optimum multiplier, not the stepsize sum S_j.
All final actual multipliers must be nonnegative if the array is to certify
an objective bound. We will prove a necessary condition even without using
those signs. Assume exact cancellation of every function-value coefficient
in the identity defining the gradient-only remainder E_j:

    ||x_start||^2-||x_end-u_j g_end||^2-2v_j f_end
            =sum lambda^(j)_ik C_ik+E_j.                  (2)

Put

    Z_j=(u_j-1)^2+v_j,
    z_j=sum_(i<n_j) h_i g_i+g_end.                         (3)

The seed may be ANY one-step array satisfying (1)--(2). Its remainder need
not be PSD. In particular this includes the primitive one-step seed in the
shared construction, with u_1=M_1,v_1=2M_1-1 and
E_1=(2-(1+a)^2)||g_0-g_1||^2.

Here is the permitted join. Write n=n_j,p=n,q=2n+1,S=S_j,
d=d_j,b=1+d,M=M_j. Choose parameters A,B,P,Q with

    r=1+A+B,
    P-Q=b+(1-A)S.                                       (4)

Embed one copy of the old array on the left block and r times the same
array on the right block. For every right queried index i=p+1,...,q-1 add

    A h_i to (p,i), B h_i to (q,i),
    -(A+B)h_i to (star,i).                               (5)

Add the following six sparse corrections:

    (p,q): P,                    (q,p): Q,
    (p,star): -s_j,              (q,star): s_(j+1)-r s_j,
    (star,p): b-u_j,             (star,q): u_(j+1)-r u_j.  (6)

No restriction to H=ell=0, greedy choices of Z, PSD local terms, rank-one
child remainders, or PSD child remainders is imposed. The final objective
matrix must still be PSD on the full formal space. Nonnegative actual
coefficients, when they hold, include all the star coefficients in (1);
requiring A,B,P,Q>=0 is a sufficient way to preserve the other signs.

The desired result is a necessary condition for ANY such recursion. It is
therefore valid for every choice of its parameters that passes all actual
sign tests, including choices depending on the target horizon.

## 2. The complete local identity

Define

    t=A-B,
    H=M+d-B(M-d)-t M,
    ell=P-A,
    V=x_p-x_q-g_p+g_q.

The exact recursion for the entire gradient remainder is

    E_(j+1)=E_j(left)+r E_j(right)
       +t||V||^2+2<V,(H-ell)g_p+ell g_q>
       +(Z_j-rd^2)||g_p||^2+(rZ_j-Z_(j+1))||g_q||^2.    (7)

We verify both the value and quadratic coefficients. Write x=x_p,g=g_p,
y=x_q,e=g_q, and w=sum_(p<i<q)h_i g_i=x-bg-y. Summing (5) gives

    2AS f_p+2BS f_q-AS||g||^2-BS||e||^2
                      -2<w,A(x-g)+B(y-e)>.             (8)

This follows by expanding a summand: the coefficient of each f_i and of
each ||g_i||^2 cancels, leaving the displayed linear and bilinear terms.
The value coefficient at p in the two children plus (5)--(6) is

    -2v_j+2AS+2(P-Q)-2s_j-2(b-u_j)
       =2[(A-1)S+P-Q-b]=0.

The coefficient at q is

    -2r v_j+2BS-2(P-Q)+2(s_(j+1)-r s_j)
                         -2(u_(j+1)-r u_j)=-2v_(j+1).

This uses (1),(4) and r=1+A+B. All other values have already canceled.

For completeness, the quadratic parts of the six expressions used in (6)
are respectively

    C_pq: 2<e,y-x>-||g-e||^2,
    C_qp: 2<g,x-y>-||g-e||^2,
    C_pstar: -||g||^2,       C_qstar: -||e||^2,
    C_starp: 2<g,x>-||g||^2,
    C_starq: 2<e,y>-||e||^2.

Subtract their weighted sum and (8) from the new potential minus the two
child potentials. With D=x-y, all dependence on the separate x,y cancels.
The resulting coefficients, using the convention that cross coefficients
multiply twice the stated inner product, are

    ||D||^2: t,
    <D,g>: H-ell-t,               <D,e>: ell+t,
    ||g||^2: Z_j-rd^2+t-2H+2ell,
    ||e||^2: rZ_j-Z_(j+1)+t+2ell,
    <g,e>: H-2ell-t.

Substituting V=D-g+e gives (7). Thus (7) concerns the full original
gradient space, not only a condensed boundary form.

## 3. A formal template on which all mixed terms vanish

Define nonzero signed scalars

    k_1=2+a=M_1,
    k_(j+1)=-k_j^2/d_j,       j>=1.                       (9)

For each block H_j and each vector e, there is an explicit formal gradient
assignment, linear in e, with terminal gradient e such that

    z_j=k_j e,
    E_j=(k_j^2-Z_j)||e||^2.                              (10)

Crucially this is true for EVERY parameter choice in (4)--(6). The same
template (9) works after a change of the storage variables, multiplier
weights, or mixed boundary terms.

First check the seed carefully. From (1)--(2), subtraction of the star
terms gives the intrinsic identity

    E_j=2sum_(i<n_j)h_i(f_i-f_end)
          +sum_(i<n_j)h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2
          +(M_j-Z_j)||g_end||^2
          -sum_(i!=k finite)lambda^(j)_ik C_ik.          (11)

To obtain (11), use
||x_start||^2-||x_end||^2
 =2sum h_i<g_i,x_i>-sum h_i^2||g_i||^2
and s_j=S_j+u_j-v_j. This also verifies that no hypothesis s_j=0 is needed.

Assign both seed gradients equal to e, and set the formal values
f_i=<e,x_i>-||e||^2/2. Every finite C_ik is then zero. More generally,
for a constant-gradient block with prefix sums P_i,
f_i-f_end=(S_j-P_i)||e||^2 and

    2sum h_i(S_j-P_i)+sum h_i(1-h_i)=S_j(S_j+1).

The latter equality follows from
sum_i h_i sum_(t>=i)h_t=(S_j^2+sum_i h_i^2)/2.
Equation (11) therefore evaluates to (M_j^2-Z_j)||e||^2.
For H_1, z_1=(2+a)e=k_1 e, proving (10). The auxiliary values merely
evaluate a polynomial identity; they are not claimed to be feasible jets.

Suppose (10) holds at level j. In the right child use its template with
terminal vector e. In the left child use its template with terminal vector

    g=-k_j e/d_j.

The block displacement gives exactly

    V=d_j g+z_right=0,
    z_parent=z_left+d_j g+z_right=z_left=k_j g=k_(j+1)e.  (12)

Evaluating the ENTIRE right side of (7), rather than discarding any child
part, now gives

    E_(j+1)
      =(k_j^2-Z_j)||g||^2+r(k_j^2-Z_j)||e||^2
           +(Z_j-rd_j^2)||g||^2+(rZ_j-Z_(j+1))||e||^2
      =[(k_j^2-rd_j^2)k_j^2/d_j^2+r k_j^2-Z_(j+1)]||e||^2
      =(k_j^4/d_j^2-Z_(j+1))||e||^2.

This is (10) at the parent. Neither E_j>=0 nor Z_j=k_j^2 was used.
In particular reducing Z at an earlier join does not remove the test:
its resulting positive template energy is carried exactly into the next
calculation and cancels with that join's storage terms.

## 4. Even changing the final initial-distance coefficient cannot repair it

Write u=u_m,v=v_m>0,k=k_m,Z=(u-1)^2+v. The full objective certificate
generated by the final array, at any proposed level q_cert, is

    M_cert=q_cert||x_0||^2-2f_n
                         +sum_(i!=j) (2lambda^(m)_ji/v) Q_ij
       =(q_cert-1/v)||x_0||^2
                         +[||x_n-u g_n||^2+E_m]/v.       (13)

The whole formula follows from (2) and C_ij=-2Q_ji. Its actual
interpolation multipliers are nonnegative when the lambda array is.

Apply (10), keep the initial formal vector x_0 independent, and write e
for the terminal template vector. Since z_m=k e,

    x_n-u g_n=x_0-(k+u-1)e.

Set beta=k+u-1. The restriction of v M_cert to the two independent
scalar coordinates in x_0,e has matrix

                    [ q_cert v      -beta       ]
                    [   -beta       2k beta-v   ].       (14)

Indeed its second diagonal is
beta^2+k^2-Z=2k(k+u-1)-v. Full PSD requires (14) PSD.
Its lower diagonal cannot be zero: PSD would then force beta=0, making
that diagonal -v<0. Consequently 2k beta-v>0, and its determinant gives

    q_cert >= beta^2/[v(2k beta-v)] >= 1/k^2.             (15)

For the last inequality use the exact identity

    k^2 beta^2-v(2k beta-v)=(v-k beta)^2>=0.

Both denominators are positive. Thus (15) holds whether E_m is PSD or
indefinite, whether q_cert equals 1/v or is increased, and whether any
child storage was selected greedily. All nonlinear mixing parameters in
(7) have vanished from the necessary bound.

This is a full-space certificate obstruction. Passing the test on legal
sampled trajectories alone would not suffice, since the formal gradient
assignment used in (10) is generally not a legal transcript.

## 5. Quantitative growing-horizon consequence for the assigned words

Fix an integer m>=128, set epsilon=1/m, lambda=1+sqrt(2), and use the
actual assignment

    c=lambda(1+epsilon), a=(1-epsilon)/c,
    d_j=(1-epsilon)c^(j-1), 1<=j<=m.

The symbol d_m is used only to express the recurrence solution; H_m uses
the bridges d_0,...,d_(m-1). Put

    R=(2+a)/[c(1-epsilon)].

Solving (9) in absolute value gives

    |k_j|=(1-epsilon)c^j R^(2^(j-1)),   1<=j<=m.          (16)

To check (16), divide |k_j| by c d_j. This ratio is R at j=1 and
is squared at every next step, since d_(j+1)=c d_j.

There is a strict gap below one in R, with an explicit uniform constant:

    1-R = epsilon[2-lambda^2 epsilon(1+epsilon)]
                  /[lambda^2(1+epsilon)^2(1-epsilon)]
          > epsilon/4.                                  (17)

For the equality multiply by c^2(1-epsilon). Its numerator is
lambda^2(1+epsilon)^2(1-epsilon)
 -[lambda^2+(2lambda-1)epsilon]
 =2epsilon-lambda^2 epsilon^2(1+epsilon),
using lambda^2=2lambda+1. For the inequality, lambda^2<6 and
epsilon<=1/128 imply

    2-lambda^2 epsilon(1+epsilon)>7/4,
    lambda^2(1+epsilon)^2(1-epsilon)<7.

Also R>0 from its definition. The bound 1-x<=exp(-x), proved for
0<=x<1 by integrating the derivative of log(1-x), now yields

    |k_m| < c^m exp[-2^(m-3)/m].                         (18)

Here c<5/2: sqrt(2)<10/7 gives
c<(17/7)(129/128)=2193/896<5/2. Combining (15),(18) gives

    q_cert > (2/5)^(2m) exp[2^(m-2)/m] -> infinity.       (19)

The limit follows because 2^m/m^2 tends to infinity: its successive ratio
is 2m^2/(m+1)^2>3/2 for all m>=7. In particular the exponential term in
the logarithm of (19) dominates the linear term 2m log(5/2).

Already for every m>=128, (18) gives |k_m|<1 and hence q_cert>1.
For an explicit elementary comparison, exp(1)>1+1+1/2+1/6>5/2, so
log(5/2)<1, while 2^(m-3)>m^2 for every m>=16 by checking m=16
and noting that 2m^2/(m+1)^2>1 thereafter. Thus the exponent in (18),
after bounding c^m by exp(m), is negative.

No upper coefficient from this family can therefore approach the small
objective levels needed for a comparison with the exact Zhang--Jiang
profile. This is stronger than failure of the previously chosen H=ell=0
rule: all the mixed terms, all retained child matrices, all earlier
storage choices, and arbitrary q_cert were included in the derivation.

## 6. Scope and the surviving repair directions

The obstruction covers exactly the repeated-copy array (4)--(6), started
from any H_1 seed with the star pattern (1). The identity requires neither
PSD child remainders nor local PSD checks, and the final test includes x_0
and every original gradient coordinate. It also permits nonzero incoming
terminal optimum weights s_j and arbitrary u_j,v_j consistent with (1).

It does NOT exclude another complete interpolation array for H_m. In
particular C51's finite H_3 certificate uses a different unrestricted
array, and is consistent with this result. The obstruction also does not
automatically cover independent internal coefficient changes at a parent,
additional interpolation edges or corrections outside (5)--(6), a
different star pattern, or a different seed size allowed to grow with m.

The concrete repair requirement is now an algebraic one: a useful changed
array must act nontrivially on the template (10)--(12). Altering only the
local V-dependent terms cannot do that. Nor can changing Z alone, since
the precise earlier template energy is propagated in (10).

For literature context, the gluing-plus-rank-one-plus-sparse architecture
is present in Altschuler--Parrilo, arXiv:2309.16530v1, Theorem 3.4 and its
proof in Sections 3.1--3.2. The exact paper was downloaded and read. No
statement from that paper is imported into this proof, and its certificate
for the undeformed silver schedule is not claimed to apply after the
present parameter deformation.

All new claims here remain candidates until verifier acceptance. The
original unrestricted target remains unresolved.

## 7. Changing only a fixed or moderately growing seed also fails

The one-step starting point is not essential. Permit an arbitrary seed
certificate for the entire block H_r, 1<=r<=m, with the same star pattern
(1), all its finite interpolation entries free, and any gradient remainder.
Above that seed use the repeated-copy join (4)--(6).

By (11), the constant-gradient assignment on the seed gives

    z_r=M_r e, E_r=(M_r^2-Z_r)||e||^2.

Start the template with k_r=M_r and repeat (12). Every subsequent identity
and the full-matrix lower bound (15) remain valid. The exact solution is

    |k_m|=(1-epsilon)c^m R_r^(2^(m-r)),
    R_r=M_r/(c d_r).

In fact R_r<=R_1 for EVERY r>=1 at the assigned parameters. To see this,
the mass recurrence M_(j+1)=2M_j+a c^j gives

    M_j=A c^j+(1-A)2^j, A=a/(c-2),
    R_j=[A+(1-A)(2/c)^j]/(1-epsilon).

One has 0<A<1: a>0,c>2, and

    c(c-2)-(1-epsilon)
       =(2lambda+3)epsilon+lambda^2 epsilon^2>0.

Thus the displayed R_j is decreasing. Combining with (17) gives the uniform
seed-size estimate

    |k_m|<c^m exp[-2^(m-r)/(4m)],
    q_cert>(2/5)^(2m) exp[2^(m-r-1)/m].                   (20)

Consequently no such certificate sequence can provide a bounded useful
upper level whenever 2^(m-r_m)/m^2 tends to infinity. This includes every
fixed seed depth and, more strongly, every choice

    r_m<=m-3 ceil(log_2 m)

for all sufficiently large m. The conclusion permits a different completely
free seed array at each horizon; it is not a finite-library assumption.

A correction confined to a fixed H_3 or H_4 seed therefore cannot repair
the growing certificate. A useful modification must continue to act at
growing recursion levels, or leave this repeated-copy architecture.

## 8. An explicit violated pair and an actual array change outside the family

This section independently checks that the template is not a legal
adversary, and exhibits a concrete multiplier direction that the previous
obstruction does not cover. It does NOT assert full PSD after that change.

For the original one-step template define b0=1+a and M1=2+a. At every
one-step leaf its two gradients coincide. Moreover all leaf endpoints have
the same proximal point Y=x_end-g_end: (12) says the left and right child
endpoints have equal proximal points, so induction propagates the common
value to every leaf. A leaf with gradient w thus has endpoint Y+w and
start Y+M1 w.

Look at the rightmost H_3 copy, which has terminal template vector e. Put

    T=M1^2/(d_1 d_2)>0.

Its left H_2 endpoint gradient is T e, while its last one-step leaf has
gradient e. In the full word these samples are j=n_m-4 (the earlier
endpoint) and i=n_m-1 (the last queried point). Hence

    g_j=T e, x_j=Y+T e,
    g_i=e,   x_i=Y+M1 e.

Adding the two interpolation expressions eliminates values exactly:

    C_ij+C_ji
       =2<g_i-g_j,x_i-x_j>-2||g_i-g_j||^2
       =2b0(1-T)||e||^2.                                (21)

For m>=128 the actual parameters have T>8/5. Indeed M1>2,
d_1 d_2=lambda(1-epsilon)(1-epsilon^2)<lambda<5/2, so
T>4/(5/2)=8/5. Thus (21) is strictly negative for e!=0.
Every legal smooth-convex transcript has both C_ij and C_ji nonnegative,
independently of the choice of function values. This gives an explicit
violated pair; no choice of values or optimum can turn this template into
an actual lower witness.

There is also a concrete, sign-preserving change of the multiplier array:
add gamma>=0 to both lambda_ij and lambda_ji. It preserves every value
flow and every star coefficient. The gradient remainder changes by

    -gamma(C_ij+C_ji)
      =2gamma[||g_i-g_j||^2-<g_i-g_j,x_i-x_j>].           (22)

On the template, the lower-right entry in (14) increases by
2gamma b0(T-1). For ANY prescribed q_cert>0 and v>0, set beta=k+u-1
as before and choose

    gamma=max(0,
       [beta^2/(q_cert v)-(2k beta-v)]/[2b0(T-1)]).        (23)

This makes that particular two-variable restriction PSD, by its Schur
complement. The increment is a real nonnegative interpolation multiplier
change, not a projection of a negative eigenvalue or a free PSD addition.

The complete matrix remains to be controlled. In fact the update (22)
itself is indefinite: setting only g_i nonzero gives a positive value,
whereas setting only g_j nonzero gives
2gamma(1-h_j)||g_j||^2<0 when gamma>0, since h_j=1+d_2>1.
This explicitly prevents treating (23) as a full certificate repair.

The indices i,j are queried samples, so the change introduces no incoming
terminal edge. The pair is already present in the unrestricted model of
accepted fact1252b8823bb5a290; it was the recursive parameterization that
failed to allow this independent change. Equation (20) also shows why a
useful implementation cannot stop at correcting only a bounded seed.

An exact extension for further work is to add, at each growing join, both
edges between p and q-1. Write w=g_(q-1), so x_(q-1)=x_q+b0 w. Its local
remainder update is

    2gamma<g_p-w,g_q+a w-V>,                             (24)

obtained by substituting x_p-x_(q-1)=V+g_p-g_q-b0 w in (22).
This term depends on the last queried gradient separately from z_block and
g_end. A boundary state that retains (z_block,g_end,g_last_query) can
represent (24); the two-variable state alone does not retain that data.
Choosing gamma and the old join parameters with full PSD at every growing
level remains an OPEN construction problem, not a conclusion of this file.

## 9. A concrete three-variable starting state for the surviving construction

The added pair can be represented with an exact, attained boundary state;
there is no need to assume an unspecified positive child remainder. The
following formulas initialize such an analytic attempt at H_2 for the
actual parameters m>=128.

Write b0=1+a,M=1+b0,d=d_1,x=d/M,eta=x^2+2x-1, and

    a0=(2-b0^2)/b0^2.

Then 1<b0<sqrt(2), 3/8<x<sqrt(2)-1, and eta<0. For the upper bound on x,
put s=sqrt(2)-1 and compute
d-sM=-epsilon[1-2s^2/(1+epsilon)]<0. For the lower bound use
d>=127/128 and M<5/2. Thus a0>0.

Start from the seed in Section 1. At the H_1-to-H_2 join set

    A=1/x, B=2+x, r=3+x+1/x, t=1/x-2-x,
    P=(1+M eta)/x, Q=M,
    u_2=M2=2M+d, v_2=2M2-1.

All actual multipliers are nonnegative: t>0, A,B,Q>0, and
1+M eta>=1-35/128>0 since eta>=-7/64 and M<5/2. Both incoming
optimum weights s_1,s_2 are zero, and the corrections (5)--(6) preserve
the star pattern as already checked in Section 2.

For H_2 gradients g0,g1,g2,g3 put

    U=b0(g0-g1), V0=b0(g2-g3), D0=g1-g3.

Substitution in (7) gives the complete remainder

    E_2=a0||U||^2+(r a0+t)||V0||^2+M^2(-eta)||D0||^2.   (25)

To check the substitution, these parameter choices give
H=-t(M+d), ell=-tM, Z_1=M^2,Z_2=M2^2. In the variables
V=d g1+z_right and g1,g3, the cross coefficient between V0 and D0
is A d-M=0, and the D0 diagonal is M^2(1-2x-x^2)=M^2(-eta).
Together with the exact two seed remainders this gives (25).

Retain the three boundary vectors (z,e,w)=(z_2,g3,g2). They are independent
coordinates because b0>0 allows g0 to vary z while holding g2,g3 fixed.
The relation is

    z-M2 e=U+V0+(M+d)D0.

The SHARP attained boundary form obtained by minimizing (25) over g0,g1 is

    K_2(z,e,w)
       =A2||z-M2 e-b0(w-e)||^2+B2||w-e||^2,
    A2=[1/a0+(1+x)^2/(-eta)]^(-1),
    B2=(r a0+t)b0^2.                                    (26)

For exact attainment and the remaining PSD part, put c0=M^2(-eta),l=M+d.
The scalar/vector square identity

    a0||U||^2+c0||D0||^2
      =[a0 c0/(c0+a0 l^2)]||U+lD0||^2
          +||a0 l U-c0 D0||^2/(c0+a0 l^2)

proves (26), and the second square can be set to zero for any supplied
boundary data. In particular (26) is not a test confined to a primal span.

At a subsequent modified join let a sharp child form be K(z,e,w). The
complete six-boundary-variable form before eliminating internal variables is

    K(L,g,u)+r K(R,e,w)
      +t||d g+R||^2+2<d g+R,(H-ell)g+ell e>
      +(Z-rd^2)||g||^2+(rZ-Znext)||e||^2
      +2gamma<g-w,e+a w-d g-R>.                         (27)

Here u is the left last-query gradient; w is the right last-query gradient,
and the parent boundary is (z,e,w), z=L+d g+R. All earlier internal PSD
squares are retained, with the right ones multiplied by r>0. Replace
L by z-d g-R and eliminate (g,u,R). Full PSD requires the eliminated
3x3 block to be PSD, its cross columns to lie in its range, and its Schur
complement in (z,e,w) to be PSD. If the eliminated block is positive
definite, this is the explicit update K_next=C-B^T A_elim^(-1)B, where
A_elim,B,C are the coefficient blocks of (27) in the indicated order.
If it is singular the range condition is essential and cannot be omitted.

Equations (26)--(27), gamma>=0, the actual coefficient signs from (4)--(6),
and the new balanced pair give a definite growing-horizon construction
problem. A sufficient target is a final v_m>=M_m, E_m>=0; (13) would then
give q_m=1/v_m<=1/M_m. No choice of all the parameters establishing this
target is asserted here. The point of (26)--(27) is to retain the variable
on which the missing real interpolation pair acts, instead of repeating
the excluded two-boundary recurrence.
